*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  UPDATE_WORK_AREA INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE update_work_area INPUT.

  w_ucomm = sy-ucomm.

ENDMODULE.                 " UPDATE_WORK_AREA INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_9000  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_9000 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc, f10, f12.

  IF sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGR' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_FTCTL'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'MGRGUIDE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_RPTS'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'USERGUIDE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_PY'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'USERGUIDE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'RPTGUIDE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'RPTGUIDE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'USERGUIDE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'MGRGUIDE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'RPTGUIDE'.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'GAOGUIDE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'USERGUIDE'.
  ENDIF.

  SET PF-STATUS 'USER_MENU' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  IF sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.
    SET TITLEBAR  '9000GAO'.
  ELSE.
    SET TITLEBAR  '9000'.
  ENDIF.
  CLEAR sy-ucomm.

  "Instantiate the variables FLEX_CONTAINER and SUP_TREE
  IF user_tree IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT user_tree_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'USER_TREE_CONTAINER'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    CREATE OBJECT user_tree
      EXPORTING
        parent                      = user_tree_container
        node_selection_mode         = cl_gui_simple_tree=>node_sel_mode_single
      EXCEPTIONS
        lifetime_error              = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        failed                      = 4
        illegal_node_selection_mode = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Define the required events
    event-eventid = cl_gui_simple_tree=>eventid_node_double_click.
    event-appl_event = abap_true.   " process PAI if event occurs
    APPEND event TO events.

    event-eventid = cl_gui_simple_tree=>eventid_node_context_menu_req.
    event-appl_event = ' '. " no PAI if event occurs
    APPEND event TO events.

    "expand no children
    event-eventid = cl_gui_simple_tree=>eventid_expand_no_children.
    event-appl_event = abap_true.
    APPEND event TO events.

    "process PAI if context menu select event occurs
    CALL METHOD user_tree->set_ctx_menu_select_event_appl
      EXPORTING
        appl_event = abap_true.

    "Register the events
    CALL METHOD user_tree->set_registered_events
      EXPORTING
        events                    = events
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                = 1
        cntl_system_error         = 2
        illegal_event_combination = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    SET HANDLER zcl_tree_event=>handle_user_double_click FOR user_tree.
    SET HANDLER zcl_tree_event=>handle_user_context_menu_req
                FOR user_tree.
    SET HANDLER zcl_tree_event=>handle_user_context_menu_sel
                FOR user_tree.
    SET HANDLER zcl_tree_event=>handle_user_expand_no_children
                FOR user_tree.

* Determine the user's access
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>determine_user_group
      IMPORTING
        e_user_group = g_user_group
        e_note_code  = g_note_code.

    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>determine_meet_update
      EXPORTING
        i_user_group  = g_user_group
      IMPORTING
        e_meet_update = g_update_m_range.

    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>determine_meet_display
      EXPORTING
        i_user_group   = g_user_group
      IMPORTING
        e_meet_display = g_display_m_range.

    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>determine_doc_update
      EXPORTING
        i_user_group = g_user_group
      IMPORTING
        e_cat_update = g_update_d_range.

    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>determine_doc_display
      EXPORTING
        i_user_group  = g_user_group
      IMPORTING
        e_cat_display = g_display_d_range.

    PERFORM initialize_user_tree.

    PERFORM add_nodes_user.

    "Expand the top node
    CALL METHOD user_tree->expand_node
      EXPORTING
        node_key = 'Root'.


    "Screen Header processing
    IF do IS INITIAL.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.
        g_text = 'Workers Compensation'.
      ELSE.
        g_text = 'Online Injury Management'.
      ENDIF.
      CREATE OBJECT do.
      CALL METHOD do->new_line.
      CALL METHOD do->add_text
        EXPORTING
          text          = g_text
          sap_style     = 'heading'
          sap_fontstyle = cl_dd_document=>serif.        " Times
*         SAP_FONTSTYLE = cl_dd_document=>sans_serif.   " Arial

      "display document
      CALL METHOD do->display_document
        EXPORTING
          container          = 'SCR_HEAD'
        EXCEPTIONS
          html_display_error = 1.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                         CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                  w_user_name.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = w_user_pernr.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_advisor.
    ENDIF.
    w_user_role = zim_advisor-role.


  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_9000  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_9600  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_9600 OUTPUT.

  SET PF-STATUS '9600' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  SET TITLEBAR  '9600'.

  CLEAR : w_code2, w_canc.


  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF doc_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT doc_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'DOC_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT doc_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = doc_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .
    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_data_changed_9600
                FOR doc_sel_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_9600
                FOR doc_sel_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_hotspot_9600
                FOR doc_sel_alv.
    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.


    "provide a title
*   doc_sel_layout-grid_title = 'Additional Files'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    doc_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    doc_sel_layout-no_rowmark = ' '.
*   prev_sel_layout-no_totline = abap_true.
    doc_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
*   doc_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    doc_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    doc_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_doc TABLES doc_sel_fieldcat.

*   PERFORM drop_down_list.

    REFRESH doc_sel_style.
    CLEAR   doc_sel_style.

    PERFORM get_add_file.

    PERFORM exclude_functions TABLES doc_sel_functions.

    CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->set_ready_for_input
      EXPORTING
        i_ready_for_input = '1'.

    CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
    CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = doc_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = doc_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = doc_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab18.

  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_9600  OUTPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  exit_9000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE exit_9000 INPUT.

  w_code = sy-ucomm.

  CASE sy-ucomm.
    WHEN 'BACK' OR 'CANC' OR 'EXIT'.
      SET SCREEN 0000.
*     CALL 'SYST_LOGOFF'.

    WHEN 'USERGUIDE'.
      IF w_user_role = 'I'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'IMA'.
      ELSEIF w_user_role = 'S'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'SIMA'.
      ELSEIF w_user_role = 'D'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'MDU'.
      ELSEIF w_user_role = 'F'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'FTC'.
      ELSEIF w_user_role = 'L'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'CSA'.
      ELSEIF w_user_role = 'T'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'FTC'.
      ELSEIF w_user_role = 'W'.
        PERFORM display_userguide USING 'WCA'.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'MGRGUIDE'.
      PERFORM display_userguide USING 'MGR'.

    WHEN 'RPTGUIDE'.
      PERFORM display_userguide USING 'RPT'.

    WHEN 'GAOGUIDE'.
      PERFORM display_userguide USING 'GAO'.

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " exit_9000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_1000  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_1000 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
    SET TITLEBAR '1000' WITH 'Display an Incident'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE'.
  ELSEIF w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
    SET TITLEBAR '1000' WITH 'Update an Incident'.
  ELSEIF w_1000_action = 'UNM'.
    SET TITLEBAR '1000' WITH 'Convert a Near Miss'.
  ELSEIF w_1000_action = 'HIS'.
    SET TITLEBAR '1000' WITH 'Create a Historical Incident'.
  ELSE.
    SET TITLEBAR '1000' WITH 'Create an Incident'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'P902'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'P902N'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DTLEARN'.
  ENDIF.
* IF w_unsworn_claim EQ space.
*   PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DTLEARN'.
* ENDIF.
  SET PF-STATUS '1000' EXCLUDING exclfunc.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_1000  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_1001  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_1001 OUTPUT.

  IF gen_txt_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT gen_txt_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'INC_TEXT_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT gen_txt_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = gen_txt_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 65
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the status bar
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_statusbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        statusbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
      EXPORTING
        visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.


    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_enable
      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
       w_readonly    = abap_true OR
       w_no_pernr IS NOT INITIAL.
      CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
        EXPORTING
          readonly_mode          = 1
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt1
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp        = 1
        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt1 LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines GT 0.
      IF w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
         w_readonly    = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->go_to_line
          EXPORTING
            line                   = w_lines
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            OTHERS                 = 2.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
      IF w_lines GT 0.
        CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->protect_lines
          EXPORTING
            from_line              = 1
            to_line                = w_lines
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            invalid_parameter      = 2
            OTHERS                 = 3.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
     w_readonly    = abap_true OR
     w_no_pernr IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
      EXPORTING
        readonly_mode          = 1
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_cntl_call_method = 1
        invalid_parameter      = 2
        OTHERS                 = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
      EXPORTING
        readonly_mode          = 0
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_cntl_call_method = 1
        invalid_parameter      = 2
        OTHERS                 = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_1001  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_1100  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_1100 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF g_user_group = 'C' OR
     g_user_group = 'D' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_IMA_DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'COPY1'.
  ENDIF.

  SET TITLEBAR  '1100'.
  SET PF-STATUS '1100' EXCLUDING exclfunc.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF inc_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT inc_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'INC_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT inc_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = inc_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_double_click_1100
                FOR inc_sel_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_hotspot_1100
                FOR inc_sel_alv.

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
*   inc_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    inc_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    inc_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    inc_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    inc_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_incident TABLES inc_sel_fieldcat.

*   perform f4_list_7500.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES inc_sel_functions.

    REFRESH inc_sel_style.
    CLEAR   inc_sel_style.

*    CALL METHOD frloc_sel_alv->set_ready_for_input
*      EXPORTING
*        i_ready_for_input = '1'.
*
*    CALL METHOD frloc_sel_alv->register_edit_event
*      EXPORTING
*        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
*    CALL METHOD frloc_sel_alv->register_edit_event
*      EXPORTING
*        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.
*
    CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = inc_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = inc_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = inc_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab3.

    CLEAR : w_1100_refresh.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_1100_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_incident.

    CLEAR w_1100_refresh.
    CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = inc_sel_layout.
    inc_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = inc_sel_layout.
    CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'V'.
*   PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'P902'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.

    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPDOC'.

    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'RESEND'.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_2102  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_2102 OUTPUT.

  IF g_user_group NE 'V'.
    IF emp_txt_text IS INITIAL.

      CREATE OBJECT emp_txt_container
        EXPORTING
          container_name = 'EMP_TEXT_CONT'.

      CREATE OBJECT emp_txt_text
        EXPORTING
          parent                     = emp_txt_container
          wordwrap_mode              = 2
          wordwrap_position          = 100
          wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


      "Hide the toolbar
      CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
        EXPORTING
          toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

      "Hide the status bar
      CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_statusbar_mode
        EXPORTING
          statusbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

      "Hide the toolbar
      CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
        EXPORTING
          visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.


      CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_enable
        EXPORTING
          enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
        EXCEPTIONS
          cntl_error        = 1
          cntl_system_error = 2
          OTHERS            = 3.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
      ENDIF.

      IF w_2100_action = 'DISP' OR
         w_readonly    = abap_true.
        CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
          EXPORTING
            readonly_mode          = 1
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            invalid_parameter      = 2
            OTHERS                 = 3.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
        EXPORTING
          table           = gt_outtxt2
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_dp        = 1
          error_dp_create = 2
          OTHERS          = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.

      IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP'.
        DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt2 LINES w_lines.

        CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->go_to_line
          EXPORTING
            line                   = w_lines
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            OTHERS                 = 2.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ENDIF.

        SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
        IF w_lines GT 0.
          CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->protect_lines
            EXPORTING
              from_line              = 1
              to_line                = w_lines
            EXCEPTIONS
              error_cntl_call_method = 1
              invalid_parameter      = 2
              OTHERS                 = 3.

          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

*    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_text_as_stream
*      EXPORTING
*        text = gt_outtxt2
*      EXCEPTIONS
**   error_dp =1
*        error_dp_create = 2.
*
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_2102  OUTPUT

        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      CALL METHOD mgr_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
        EXPORTING
          table           = gt_outtxt3
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_dp        = 1
          error_dp_create = 2
          OTHERS          = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.

      IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP'.
        DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt3 LINES w_lines.

        CALL METHOD mgr_txt_text->go_to_line
          EXPORTING
            line                   = w_lines
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            OTHERS                 = 2.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ENDIF.

        SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
        IF w_lines GT 0.
          CALL METHOD mgr_txt_text->protect_lines
            EXPORTING
              from_line              = 1
              to_line                = w_lines
            EXCEPTIONS
              error_cntl_call_method = 1
              invalid_parameter      = 2
              OTHERS                 = 3.

          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_2103  OUTPUT

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF med_prac_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT med_prac_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'MED_PRAC_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT med_prac_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = med_prac_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_double_click_2105
                FOR med_prac_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_2105
                FOR med_prac_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_data_changed_2105
                FOR med_prac_alv.

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    med_prac_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    med_prac_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    med_prac_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    med_prac_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    med_prac_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_med_prac TABLES med_prac_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES med_prac_functions.

    REFRESH med_prac_style.
    CLEAR   med_prac_style.

    CALL METHOD med_prac_alv->set_ready_for_input
      EXPORTING
        i_ready_for_input = '1'.

    CALL METHOD med_prac_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
    CALL METHOD med_prac_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD med_prac_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = med_prac_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = med_prac_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = med_prac_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab6.

  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_2105  OUTPUT

    CALL METHOD med_prac_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = med_prac_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = med_prac_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = med_prac_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab6.

  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_2205  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_2207  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_2207 OUTPUT.

  IF emp_txt_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT emp_txt_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'EMP_TEXT_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT emp_txt_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = emp_txt_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 100
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the status bar
    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_statusbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        statusbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
      EXPORTING
        visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.


    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_enable
      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
      EXPORTING
        readonly_mode          = 1
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_cntl_call_method = 1
        invalid_parameter      = 2
        OTHERS                 = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD emp_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt2
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp        = 1
        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_2207  OUTPUT

MODULE status_2209 OUTPUT.
* Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF clm_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT clm_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'CLM_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

* create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT clm_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = clm_sel_container
        i_appl_events = 'X'.

*--Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

*--Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3404
                FOR clm_sel_alv.

*--Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

* provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

* inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    clm_sel_layout-no_toolbar = 'X'.
    clm_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    clm_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    clm_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    clm_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_clm_sel_pre88 TABLES clm_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES clm_sel_functions.

    REFRESH clm_sel_style.
    CLEAR   clm_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = clm_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = clm_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = clm_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab30.

    w_2209_refresh = 'X'.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_2209_refresh = 'X'.
    PERFORM get_claim_status.

    CLEAR w_2209_refresh.
    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = clm_sel_layout.
    clm_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = clm_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_2209  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3000 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  SET TITLEBAR  '3000'.

  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP6'.
  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP7'.
  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPDLEG'.

  IF g_user_group = 'C' OR
     g_user_group = 'V' OR
     g_user_group = 'D'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'V'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPDOC'.
  ENDIF.


  SET PF-STATUS '3000' EXCLUDING exclfunc.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF case_act_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT case_act_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'CASE_ACT_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT case_act_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = case_act_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
*   INIT_ASS_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    case_act_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    case_act_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    case_act_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    case_act_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_case_act TABLES case_act_fieldcat
                                    USING  'X'.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES case_act_functions.

    REFRESH case_act_style.
    CLEAR   case_act_style.

    PERFORM get_case_act.

    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = case_act_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = case_act_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = case_act_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab7.

    CLEAR w_3000_refresh.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_case_act.

    CLEAR w_3000_refresh.
    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = case_act_layout.
    case_act_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = case_act_layout.
    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3000  OUTPUT

        it_toolbar_excluding = mc2_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = mc2_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab31.

    CLEAR w_3000_refresh.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_mc_ben.

    CLEAR w_3000_refresh.
    CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = mc2_sel_layout.
    mc2_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = mc2_sel_layout.
    CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3010  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3010 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF g_user_group = 'S'.
    SET TITLEBAR  '3010PRE88'.
  ELSE.
    SET TITLEBAR  '3010'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGR'    AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRCM'  AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_FTCTL'  AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRDEP' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRSCU' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRCS'  AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_SIMA'   AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRDEP'.   "CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'EDITNOTES'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DELDOC'.
  ENDIF.


  IF sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_CM' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD4A'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD5'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD6'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP4A'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP5'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD9'. " AZORBA EPSS BRD 05
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP9'. " AZORBA EPSS BRD 05
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_CM' OR
     sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_WC' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MC'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'EDITNOTES'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPDOC'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD6'.

  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'D' OR
     sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_IMA_DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'C'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group NE 'S'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD8'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP8'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'S'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'V'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD3'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'CLOSE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPDOC'.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP6'.
  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP7'.
  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DEPACT'.
  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DEPREF'.

  SET PF-STATUS '3010' EXCLUDING exclfunc.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF case_act_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT case_act_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'CASE_ACT_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT case_act_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = case_act_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
*   INIT_ASS_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    case_act_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    case_act_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    case_act_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    case_act_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.
    case_act_layout-ctab_fname = 'CELL_COLOUR'.


    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM' OR
       sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_case_act_cm TABLES case_act_fieldcat.
    ELSEIF g_user_group = 'S'.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_case_act_pre88 TABLES case_act_fieldcat
                                            USING  ' '.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_case_act TABLES case_act_fieldcat
                                      USING  ' '.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES case_act_functions.

    REFRESH case_act_style.
    CLEAR   case_act_style.

    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = case_act_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = case_act_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = case_act_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab7.

    case_act_alv->set_ready_for_input( 1 ).

    case_act_alv->register_edit_event(
    EXPORTING i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified ).

    CLEAR w_3010_refresh.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_3010_refresh = abap_true.
    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM' OR
       sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
      PERFORM get_case_act_by_emp_cm.
    ELSEIF g_user_group = 'S'.
      PERFORM get_case_act_by_emp_pre88.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM get_case_act_by_emp.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR w_3010_refresh.
    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = case_act_layout.
    case_act_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = case_act_layout.
    CALL METHOD case_act_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3010  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3100  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3100 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'REM'.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP2'.
  ENDIF.
  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_WC' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MC' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'REM'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISP2'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'NOTES1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'NOTESALL1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPDOC'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPREM'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_3100_from = 2100.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'V'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPDOC'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'NOTES1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'NOTESALL1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'REM'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DISPREM'.
  ENDIF.


  SET TITLEBAR  '3100'.
  SET PF-STATUS '3100' EXCLUDING exclfunc.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3100  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3102  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3102 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF serv_req_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT serv_req_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'SERV_REQ_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT serv_req_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = serv_req_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_hotspot_3102
                FOR serv_req_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_double_click_3102
                FOR serv_req_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3102
                FOR serv_req_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_data_changed_3102
                FOR serv_req_alv.

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    serv_req_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    serv_req_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    serv_req_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    serv_req_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    serv_req_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_serv_req TABLES serv_req_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES serv_req_functions.

    REFRESH serv_req_style.
    CLEAR   serv_req_style.

    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = serv_req_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = serv_req_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = serv_req_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab8.

    CLEAR w_3102_refresh.

  ENDIF.

  IF serv_txt_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT serv_txt_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'SERV_TXT_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT serv_txt_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = serv_txt_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 72
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the status bar
    CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->set_statusbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        statusbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
      EXPORTING
        visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.


    CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->set_enable
      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_3100_action = 'DISP' OR
       w_readonly    = abap_true.
      CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
        EXPORTING
          readonly_mode          = 1
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt6
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp        = 1
        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt6 LINES w_lines.

    CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->go_to_line
      EXPORTING
        line                   = w_lines
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_cntl_call_method = 1
        OTHERS                 = 2.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    ENDIF.

    SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
    IF w_lines GT 0.
      CALL METHOD serv_txt_text->protect_lines
        EXPORTING
          from_line              = 1
          to_line                = w_lines
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_3102_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_service_request.

    CLEAR w_3102_refresh.
    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = serv_req_layout.
    serv_req_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = serv_req_layout.

    "Set the layout depending on the Request Type
    PERFORM build_fieldcat_serv_req TABLES serv_req_fieldcat.
    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog
      EXPORTING
        it_fieldcatalog = serv_req_fieldcat.

    CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3102  OUTPUT

        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = meet_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_hotspot_3105
                FOR meet_sel_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3105
                FOR meet_sel_alv.

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    meet_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    meet_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    meet_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    meet_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    meet_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.


    IF w_4001_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_meet_sel1 TABLES meet_sel_fieldcat.
    ELSE.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_meet_sel TABLES meet_sel_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES meet_sel_functions.

    REFRESH meet_sel_style.
    CLEAR   meet_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD meet_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = meet_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = meet_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = meet_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab11.

    w_3105_refresh = abap_true.
    zim_screen_meet-meet_type_all = 'A'.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_3105_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_meeting USING zim_screen_meet-meet_type_all.

    CLEAR w_3105_refresh.
    CALL METHOD meet_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = meet_sel_layout.
    meet_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD meet_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = meet_sel_layout.
* If All Meetings are selected the layout changes
    IF w_3100_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_meet_sel TABLES meet_sel_fieldcat.
    ELSEIF w_4001_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM build_fieldcat_meet_sel1 TABLES meet_sel_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD meet_sel_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog
      EXPORTING
        it_fieldcatalog = meet_sel_fieldcat.

    CALL METHOD meet_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3202  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3202 OUTPUT.

  IF leg_txt_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT leg_txt_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'LEG_TXT_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT leg_txt_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = leg_txt_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 100
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the status bar
    CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->set_statusbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        statusbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
      EXPORTING
        visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.


    CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->set_enable
      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_3200_action = 'DISP' OR
       w_readonly    = abap_true.
      CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
        EXPORTING
          readonly_mode          = 1
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt9
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp        = 1
        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt9 LINES w_lines.

    CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->go_to_line
      EXPORTING
        line                   = w_lines
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_cntl_call_method = 1
        OTHERS                 = 2.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    ENDIF.

    SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
    IF w_lines GT 0.
      CALL METHOD leg_txt_text->protect_lines
        EXPORTING
          from_line              = 1
          to_line                = w_lines
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.




ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3202  OUTPUT

    CALL METHOD meet2_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = meet2_sel_layout.
    meet2_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD meet2_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = meet2_sel_layout.
* If All Meetings are selected the layout changes
    PERFORM build_fieldcat_meet_sel TABLES meet2_sel_fieldcat.
    CALL METHOD meet2_sel_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog
      EXPORTING
        it_fieldcatalog = meet2_sel_fieldcat.


    CALL METHOD meet2_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3205  OUTPUT

*&      Module  STATUS_3304  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3304 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF imep_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT imep_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'IMEP_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT imep_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = imep_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3304
                FOR imep_sel_alv.


    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    imep_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    imep_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    imep_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    imep_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    imep_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_imep_sel TABLES imep_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES imep_sel_functions.

    REFRESH imep_sel_style.
    CLEAR   imep_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD imep_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = imep_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = imep_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = imep_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab22.

    w_3304_refresh = abap_true.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_3304_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_imep.

    CLEAR w_3304_refresh.
    CALL METHOD imep_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = imep_sel_layout.
    imep_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD imep_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = imep_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD imep_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3304  OUTPUT

  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  ENDIF.

  SET PF-STATUS '3400' EXCLUDING exclfunc.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  MODULE screen_control_3400
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3400  OUTPUT.
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim = abap_true AND
       screen-group1 = 'SWR'.     "Hide sworn tabs
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSEIF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim IS INITIAL AND
       screen-group1 = 'UNS'.     "Hide unsworn tabs
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDMODULE.                    "screen_control_3400

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3401  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3401 OUTPUT.

  "Recalculate the current Entitlement Period Bucket.
  "This is required where the user may have changed
  "Bucket dates after choosing "Upd P905"
  "Func Group Z_OLIMS screen 8606).
  IF w_unsworn_claim EQ abap_true.
    IF w_epb_det-pernr    NE zim_in_mast-pernr OR
       w_epb_det-incident NE zim_in_mast-incident.
      PERFORM get_epb_detail.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "Redetermine Primary Claim details.
  IF w_3413_refresh EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM get_concurrent_details.
    CLEAR w_3413_refresh.
    IF prim_hist_alv IS BOUND.
      CALL METHOD prim_hist_alv->refresh_table_display.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "Redetermine External Claim details.
  IF w_3414_refresh EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM get_ecp_detail_and_history.
    CLEAR w_3414_refresh.
    IF ecp_hist_alv IS BOUND.
      CALL METHOD ecp_hist_alv->refresh_table_display.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3401  OUTPUT

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES clm_sel_functions.

    REFRESH req_sel_style.
    CLEAR   req_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD req_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = req_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = req_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = req_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab56.

    w_4103_refresh = 'X'.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_4103_refresh = 'X'.
    PERFORM get_req_review.

    CLEAR w_4103_refresh.
    CALL METHOD req_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = req_sel_layout.
    req_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    CALL METHOD req_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = req_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD req_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF w_3500_action = 'DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPDDIS'.


  SET TITLEBAR  '3500'.
  SET PF-STATUS '3500' EXCLUDING exclfunc.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3500  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_4106  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_4106 OUTPUT.

* DEVK9HRGPL
  DATA lv_p2_test_date TYPE datum.

  IF zim_in_mast-pernr IS NOT INITIAL AND zim_in_mast-incident IS NOT INITIAL.
    " Select the P1/P2 mock current dates
    SELECT SINGLE mock_current_date_p2 INTO lv_p2_test_date FROM zim_in_epss
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

    IF lv_p2_test_date IS INITIAL.
      lv_p2_test_date = sy-datum.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_epss_det-ir_date IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM calculate_ir USING lv_p2_test_date.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM calculate_ir USING w_epss_det-ir_date.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE. " STATUS_4106

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3600  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3600 OUTPUT.
* DEVK9HRGPL
  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'UPD1'.
  ENDIF.


  SET TITLEBAR  '3600'.
  SET PF-STATUS '3600' EXCLUDING exclfunc.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3600  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3602 OUTPUT.
* Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF clm_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT clm_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'CLM_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

* create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT clm_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = clm_sel_container
        i_appl_events = 'X'.

*--Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

*--Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3602
                FOR clm_sel_alv.

*--Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

* provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

* inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    clm_sel_layout-no_toolbar = 'X'.
    clm_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    clm_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    clm_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    clm_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_clm_sel_pre88a TABLES clm_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES clm_sel_functions.

    REFRESH clm_sel_style.
    CLEAR   clm_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = clm_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = clm_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = clm_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab30.

    w_3602_refresh = 'X'.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_3602_refresh = 'X'.
    PERFORM get_claim_status.

    CLEAR w_3602_refresh.
    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = clm_sel_layout.
    clm_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = clm_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3602  OUTPUT

  ENDIF.

MODULE status_3707 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF mc_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT mc_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'MC_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT mc_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = mc_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3107
                FOR mc_sel_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_data_changed_3707
                FOR mc_sel_alv.


    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    mc_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    mc_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    mc_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    mc_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    mc_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_mc_gao TABLES mc_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES mc_sel_functions.

    REFRESH mc_sel_style.
    CLEAR   mc_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
    CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = mc_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = mc_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = mc_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab19.

    w_3707_refresh = abap_true.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_3707_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_mc.
    PERFORM get_capacity.
    PERFORM get_mc_gao.

    CLEAR w_3707_refresh.
    CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = mc_sel_layout.
    mc_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = mc_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD mc_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3707  OUTPUT

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_8070  OUTPUT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_8080  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_8080 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH : exclfunc.
  CLEAR   : exclfunc.

*  if w_2000_action = 'DISP' or
*     w_readonly    = 'X'.
*    perform exclfunc_append using 'OK'.
*  endif.

  SET PF-STATUS '8080' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  SET TITLEBAR  '8080'.

  CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm, w_canc.

  IF doc_link_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT doc_link_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'DOC_LINK_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT doc_link_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = doc_link_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 80
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


*  Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD doc_link_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

*  Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD doc_link_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
      EXPORTING
        visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.

    REFRESH events.
    CLEAR events.

    event-eventid = cl_gui_textedit=>event_context_menu.
    event-appl_event = ' '.
    APPEND event TO events.

    event-eventid = cl_gui_textedit=>event_context_menu_selected.
    event-appl_event = ' '.
    APPEND event TO events.

    event-eventid = cl_gui_toolbar=>m_id_function_selected.
    event-appl_event = 'X'.
    APPEND event TO events.

    event-eventid = cl_gui_toolbar=>m_id_dropdown_clicked.
    APPEND event TO events.

* Register the events
    CALL METHOD doc_link_text->set_registered_events
      EXPORTING
        events                    = events
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                = 1
        cntl_system_error         = 2
        illegal_event_combination = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD doc_link_text->set_enable
      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD doc_link_text->set_text_as_stream
      EXPORTING
        text            = gt_outtxt80
      EXCEPTIONS
*       error_dp        =
*       =1              =
        error_dp_create = 2.


  ENDIF.



ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_8080  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  MODULE status_8090 OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_8090 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.


  "Screen Header processing
  IF do IS INITIAL.
    g_text = 'Online Injury Management'.
    CREATE OBJECT do.
    CALL METHOD do->new_line.
    CALL METHOD do->add_text
      EXPORTING
        text      = g_text
        sap_style = 'heading'.

    "display document
    CALL METHOD do->display_document
      EXPORTING
        container          = 'SCR_HEAD'
      EXCEPTIONS
        html_display_error = 1.
  ENDIF.

  SET PF-STATUS '8090' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  SET TITLEBAR  '8090'.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_8090  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_8100  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_8100 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  CLEAR  w_canc.

  IF w_8100_action = 'DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE1'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SELALL'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DELALL'.
  ENDIF.

  SET PF-STATUS '8100' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  SET TITLEBAR  '8100'.

  "Create the Work Breakdown ALV table
  IF init_con_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT init_con_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'INIT_CON_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT init_con_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = init_con_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_8100
                FOR init_con_alv.

    "provide a title
*   po_sel_layout-grid_title = 'View/Update Contractors'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    init_con_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    init_con_layout-no_rowmark = abap_true.
    init_con_layout-sel_mode   = ' '.
    init_con_layout-cwidth_opt = ' '.
    init_con_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    init_con_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_init_con TABLES init_con_fieldcat.

    PERFORM get_init_con USING w_contact_type.

    CALL METHOD init_con_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = init_con_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = init_con_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = init_con_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab5.

  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_8100  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_1000  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_1000 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_1000_action = 'DISP' AND
       screen-group4 = '100'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF ( w_1000_action = 'UPD' OR
         w_1000_action = 'UNM' OR
         w_no_pernr IS INITIAL ) AND
       screen-group3 = '100'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF p9001-identnr IS INITIAL AND
       screen-group1 = '100'.
      screen-input     = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF p9001-bp_number IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF screen-group1 = '900'.
        screen-input     = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group1 = '910'.
      screen-input     = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_1000  OUTPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_1001  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_1001 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT tb_activity INTO ls_activity
       WHERE activity_code = p9001-activity_new.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR ls_activity.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tb_cause INTO ls_cause
       WHERE cause_code = p9001-cause_new.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR ls_cause.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF ( w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
         w_no_pernr IS NOT INITIAL ) AND
       screen-group4 = '100'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Hide police fields for admin officers or
    "for Non Work Related and Near Miss
    IF screen-group1 = '200'.
      IF ( pa0001-persk GE 'XG' AND
           pa0001-persk LE 'XO' ) OR
           pa0001-persk  = 'XT'   OR
* CR3185 DEVK9HRFNJ - MD
*           pa0001-persk  = 'XX'   or
*           pa0001-persk  = 'XY'   or
* CR3185 DEVK9HRFNJ - MD
          p9001-nearmiss = '3' OR
          p9001-nearmiss = '5'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    "Hide fields linked to other fields
    IF screen-group2 = '100'.
      IF ls_activity-additional_info = 'D'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    IF screen-group2 = '110'.
      IF ls_activity-additional_info = 'M'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group2 = '120'.
      IF ls_activity-other_text IS NOT INITIAL.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group2 = '130'.
      IF ls_cause-other_text IS NOT INITIAL.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group2 = '140'.
      IF p9001-safework_not = '1'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group2 = '200'.
      IF p9001-taser = '1'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    IF screen-group2 = '300'.
      IF p9001-lbv = '1'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    IF screen-group2 = '400'.
      IF p9001-gel = '1'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    IF screen-group2 = '500'.
      IF p9001-thigh = '1'.
        screen-invisible = ' '.
        screen-active    = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-active    = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SET CURSOR FIELD w_cursor.

  CLEAR w_cursor.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_1001  OUTPUT

  LOOP AT SCREEN.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_1003  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_1003 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT tb_treatopt INTO ls_treatopt
       WHERE treat_option = p9001-treatmentopt.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR ls_treatopt.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tb_currfit INTO ls_currfit
       WHERE fitness_code = p9001-fitnessstat.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR ls_currfit.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set Treatment Person field to display
    IF screen-name = 'P9001-TREATFIRSTPER'.
      IF p9001-treatfirsta = abap_true.
        screen-input  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-input  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    "Hide the medical treatment fields
    IF screen-group3 = '100'.
      IF ls_treatopt-medical_treat IS INITIAL.
        screen-input  = '0'.
      ELSE.
        screen-input  = '1'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group2 = '110' OR
       screen-group2 = '120'.
      IF screen-group2 = '110'.
        IF ls_currfit-return_work IS NOT INITIAL.
          screen-invisible = ' '.
          screen-active    = '1'.
        ELSE.
          screen-invisible = '1'.
          screen-active    = '0'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        IF ls_currfit-med_review IS NOT INITIAL.
          screen-invisible = ' '.
          screen-active    = '1'.
        ELSE.
          screen-invisible = '1'.
          screen-active    = '0'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.


    IF p9001-identnr IS INITIAL AND
       screen-group1 = '100'.
      screen-input     = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF ( w_1000_action = 'DISP' OR
         w_no_pernr IS NOT INITIAL ) AND
       screen-group4 = '100'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SET CURSOR FIELD w_cursor.

  CLEAR w_cursor.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_1003  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_2100  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_2100 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
* Hide the tabs based on the user group

    IF screen-group3 = '010'.    " Case Details
      screen-invisible  = '0'.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '050'.    " Insurer Contact
      IF g_user_group = 'V'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '060'.    " Health Professionals
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'D'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '070'.    " Ergonomic Assessment
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'D' OR
         g_user_group = 'S' OR
         g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '080'.    " Service Request
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'D' OR
         g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '090'.    " Employee Contact (SCU)
      IF g_user_group NE 'I' OR
         zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '100'.    " Employer Contact (SCU)
      IF g_user_group NE 'I' OR
         zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY SCREEN.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_2100  OUTPUT

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_2200_action = 'DISP' AND
      screen-group1 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

* Hide the incident contact details if they are not populated
    IF screen-group2 = '100'.
      IF zim_screen_address-oth_work_external IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_work_eagle    IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_home_tel      IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_home_mobile   IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_home_email    IS INITIAL.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_2202  OUTPUT

    ENDIF.

* If Pre 88 or Non-Work Related or One-Off Assessment
    "Hide the Contact Complete button

         zim_in_mast-inc_type ='O'  OR
         zim_in_mast-inc_type ='N' ) AND
         screen-group2 = '200'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL AND
       screen-group3   = '300'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_2200_action = 'DISP' AND
      screen-group1 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
