*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  screen_control_3100  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3100 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all tabs to hide if WC, except MC and RTWP
    IF screen-group1 = '100'.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_WC' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MC'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group1 = '200'.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MC'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group1 = '300'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '0'.
    ENDIF.

    IF screen-group3 = '010'.    " Case Details
      screen-invisible  = '0'.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '040'.    " R@WP
      IF g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '050'.    " Meetings
      IF g_user_group = 'V'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '060'.    " Rehab Placement
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'S' OR
         g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '070'.    " Erg Assessment
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'S' OR
         g_user_group = 'D' OR
         g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '080'.    " Service Request
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'D' OR
         g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF screen-group3 = '090'.    " Medical Reports
      IF g_user_group = 'V' OR
         g_user_group = 'C'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY SCREEN.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDMODULE.                 " screen_control_3100  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_3101  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3101 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set the Restricted field to update for Managers only
    IF screen-group4 = '100'.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'.
        screen-input  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-input  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set the 2005 Award field to update for Managers and SIMA's
    IF screen-group4 = '200'.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SIMA' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_FTCTL'.
        screen-input  = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-input  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "display the Oath of Office for Police only
    IF w_unsworn_claim = abap_true AND
       screen-group2 = '100'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    "Display access can only see the IMA and the Incident Date
    IF g_user_group = 'V'.
      IF screen-group3 NE '100'.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.



  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3101  OUTPUT



*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_3102  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3102 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_request IS INITIAL.
      IF screen-group1 IS NOT INITIAL.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
        screen-active     = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF w_request = 'E' AND
       screen-group1 IS NOT INITIAL AND
       screen-group1 NE '100'.
      screen-invisible  = '1'.
      screen-active     = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSEIF w_request = 'R' AND
       screen-group1 IS NOT INITIAL AND
       screen-group1 NE '300'.
      screen-invisible  = '1'.
      screen-active     = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSEIF w_request = 'P' AND
       screen-group1 IS NOT INITIAL AND
       screen-group1 NE '400'.
      screen-invisible  = '1'.
      screen-active     = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3100_action = 'DISP' AND
      screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3102  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  screen_control_3300  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3300 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set Pre 88 Discharge Tab to inactive
    IF screen-group1 = '100'.
      IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set IMEP, FTC and MDU tabs to inactive
    IF screen-group1 = '200'.
      IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Only display the IMEP tab if one exists
    IF screen-group2 = '300'.
      IF w_imep_exists IS INITIAL.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    IF screen-group3 = '100'.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DEP' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

* Do not display the FTC Tab
    IF screen-group4 = '999'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

* Hide FTC Tab - MD
    IF screen-name = 'CASE_CLO_TAB10'."Tab name
      screen-active    =  '0'.
      screen-invisible =  '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.



  ENDLOOP.

ENDMODULE.                 " screen_control_3300  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_3401  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3401 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.

    "Hide new terminated employee if blank
    IF w_frem_pernr IS INITIAL AND
      screen-group1 = '500'.
      screen-input      = '0'.
      screen-invisible  = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Hide fields for Sworn / Unsworn.
    CASE screen-group2.
      WHEN '100'.
*       Hide Unsworn fields.
        IF w_unsworn_claim = abap_true.
          screen-input     = '0'.
          screen-invisible = '1'.
          MODIFY SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN '200'.
*       Hide Sworn fields.
        IF w_unsworn_claim IS INITIAL.
          screen-input     = '0'.
          screen-invisible = '1'.
          MODIFY SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN OTHERS.
    ENDCASE.
    "Set the Date of Disablement to Display
    IF w_unsworn_claim IS INITIAL.
      IF screen-group3 = '100'.
        screen-input      = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    "Set Recurring Date to Display
    IF zim_in_cm-recur IS INITIAL AND
      screen-group1 = '100'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set Planned HOurs Date to Display
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours IS INITIAL AND
      screen-group1 = '200'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

* Hide the incident contact details if they are not populated
    IF screen-group2 = '300'.
      IF zim_screen_address-oth_work_external IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_work_eagle    IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_home_tel      IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_home_mobile   IS INITIAL AND
         zim_screen_address-oth_home_email    IS INITIAL.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
* Transition to EPSS flag is only editable for IP (PBRI) claims.
    IF w_epss = abap_true AND screen-name = 'ZIM_IN_CM-TRANSITION_TO_EPSS'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03

  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3401  OUTPUT

    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_4104 OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_4104 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.

*   Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_4100_action = 'DISP' AND
      screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

*   Display Only if P2 hasn't started yet
    IF zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
      IF w_4100_action NE 'DISP' AND
        screen-group1 EQ '666'.
        screen-input  = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.


  ENDLOOP.

      screen-group3 = '300'.

      screen-invisible = '1'.

       zim_in_pre88-rec_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
      screen-group3 = '500'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS NOT INITIAL AND
      screen-group4 = '600'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_pre88-liab_sent_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
      screen-group4 = '700'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

    "Set MC Tab to hide
    IF w_gao_task = 'D' AND
      screen-group1 = '100'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set Dependent Tab to hide
    IF w_gao_task = 'M' AND
      screen-group1 = '200'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
*   Hide Upd P905 tab for Unsworn claims.
    IF w_unsworn_claim = abap_true AND
       screen-group1   = '300'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

*   Hide tabs Upd905 and Primary claim
    IF screen-group2 = 'HOD' AND
       ( zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true OR
         zim_in_mast-inc_type = 'N' ).
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_5100 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF screen-group1 = '100'.
      IF sy-tcode CS 'MGR' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SIMA'.
        screen-invisible  = '0'.
        screen-active     = '1'.
      ELSE.
        screen-invisible  = '1'.
        screen-active     = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_5100  OUTPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_7020  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_7020 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF zim_screen-user_cat IS INITIAL AND
       ( screen-group1 = '100' OR
         screen-group1 = '200' OR
         screen-group1 = '300' ).
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSEIF ( zim_screen-user_cat = 'E' OR
             zim_screen-user_cat = 'C' OR
             zim_screen-user_cat = 'S' OR
             zim_screen-user_cat = 'R' OR
             zim_screen-user_cat = 'G' ) AND
       ( screen-group1 = '100' OR
         screen-group1 = '200' ).
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'O' OR
           zim_screen-user_cat = 'U'.
      IF screen-group1 = '100'.
        screen-input  = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_code IS INITIAL AND
       ( screen-group1 = '200' OR
         screen-group1 = '300' ).
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSEIF screen-group1 = '200'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen-user_cat NE 'E'.
      IF screen-group2 = '500'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        screen-input     = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      screen-invisible = '0'.
      screen-input     = '1'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_7020  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_6000  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_6000 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  SET PF-STATUS '6000' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  SET TITLEBAR  '6000'.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF ltr_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT ltr_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'LTR_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT ltr_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = ltr_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .
    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
*   SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_double_click_1000
*               FOR ltr_sel_alv.
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_6000
                FOR ltr_sel_alv.

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    IF gt_table_changed IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.
    ENDIF.

    "provide a title
    ltr_sel_layout-grid_title =
       'Select the Correspondence to be Processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    ltr_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    ltr_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    ltr_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    ltr_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    ltr_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_ltr TABLES ltr_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM f4_list_6000.

    REFRESH ltr_sel_style.
    CLEAR   ltr_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD ltr_sel_alv->set_ready_for_input
      EXPORTING
        i_ready_for_input = '1'.

    CALL METHOD ltr_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
    CALL METHOD ltr_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD ltr_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout       = ltr_sel_layout
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog = ltr_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab       = gt_outtab14.


  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_6000  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_6100  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_6100 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  SET PF-STATUS '6100' EXCLUDING exclfunc.
  SET TITLEBAR  '6100'.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF ltr2_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT ltr2_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'LTR2_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT ltr2_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = ltr2_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .
    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    IF gt_table_changed IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.
    ENDIF.

    "provide a title
    ltr2_sel_layout-grid_title =
       'Select the Correspondence to be Sent'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    ltr2_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    ltr2_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    ltr2_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    ltr2_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    ltr2_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_ltr2 TABLES ltr2_sel_fieldcat.

    REFRESH ltr2_sel_style.
    CLEAR   ltr2_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->set_ready_for_input
      EXPORTING
        i_ready_for_input = '1'.

    CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
    CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout       = ltr2_sel_layout
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog = ltr2_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab       = gt_outtab15.


  ENDIF.

  w_3400_changed = abap_true.

  PERFORM validate_3411.

ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_3411  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHECK_SCREEN_3412  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_screen_3412 INPUT.

  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  w_3400_changed = abap_true.

* Adjust the validity periods.
  IF w_piawe_det-piawe_pre_52_wk NE w_piawe_det_old-piawe_pre_52_wk.
    IF w_piawe_det-begda_pre_52_wk IS INITIAL.
      w_piawe_det-begda_pre_52_wk = sy-datum.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_piawe_det-endda_pre_52_wk IS INITIAL.
      w_piawe_det-endda_pre_52_wk = '99991231'.
    ENDIF.
    w_piawe_det-manual_pre_52_wk = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  IF w_piawe_det-piawe_post_52_wk NE w_piawe_det_old-piawe_post_52_wk.
    IF w_piawe_det-begda_post_52_wk IS INITIAL.
      w_piawe_det-begda_post_52_wk = sy-datum.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_piawe_det-endda_post_52_wk IS INITIAL.
      w_piawe_det-endda_post_52_wk  = '99991231'.
    ENDIF.
    w_piawe_det-manual_post_52_wk = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM validate_3412.

ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_3412  INPUT

* Adjust the validity periods.

     w_prim_det-endda_cec IS NOT INITIAL.

      w_prim_det-begda_cec = sy-datum.

ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_3413  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  VALIDATE_7030  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE validate_7030 INPUT.

  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF  w_begda IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE ID 'ZZ' TYPE 'E' NUMBER '999' WITH
            'Please enter a Date From'.
  ENDIF.

  IF  w_endda IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE ID 'ZZ' TYPE 'E' NUMBER '999' WITH
            'Please enter a Date To'.
  ENDIF.

  IF  w_begda GT w_endda.
    MESSAGE ID 'ZZ' TYPE 'E' NUMBER '999' WITH
            'Date From must be less than Date To'.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " VALIDATE_7030  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  VALIDATE_7040  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE validate_7040 INPUT.

  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_new_disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_new_disablement_date LT zim_incident-dateincident.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date of Disablement cannot be'
                      'before the incident date'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_new_disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_new_disablement_date LT '20120119'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date of Disablement cannot be'
                      'less than 19/01/2012'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_dis_reason1 IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Reason the Date is being changed'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " VALIDATE_7040  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHECK_SCREEN_7050  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_screen_7050 INPUT.

  IF w_popup_ucomm = 'BACK' OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'CANC' OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'EXIT' OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'DELETE'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* To make the screen dynamic and have the other field displayed
* automatically, the tables must be read.
* But if the validations are performed, error messages will
* appear immediately i.e. enter location and the nature must
* be entered will appear.
  IF w_popup_ucomm = 'LOC'  OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'NAT'.
    PERFORM read_7050.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM validate_7050.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_7050  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_1000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1000 INPUT.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_1000_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_1000_from = 8000.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_1000_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_gen_txt.
        PERFORM free_inc_inj.
        PERFORM free_inc_email.
*       PERFORM free_inc_nat.
        IF w_1000_from NE 2100.
          PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING p9001-pernr.
        ENDIF.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM set_defaults_1000.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      PERFORM validate_1000.
      IF p9001-bp_number IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM validate_1001.
        PERFORM validate_incident_desc.
        PERFORM validate_inc_inj CHANGING w_error.
        IF w_error IS INITIAL.
          PERFORM validate_1003.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM validate_1005.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM create_incident.
        IF w_error IS INITIAL.
          SET SCREEN w_1000_from.
          PERFORM free_gen_txt.
          PERFORM free_inc_inj.
          PERFORM free_inc_nat.
          IF w_1000_from NE 2100.
            PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING p9001-pernr.
          ENDIF.
          w_1100_refresh = abap_true.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'P902N'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'N'.

    WHEN 'DTLEARN'.
      PERFORM display_detailed_earnings_rpt.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1000  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_1001  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1001 INPUT.
* set the time default
  IF w_1000_action NE 'DISP' AND
     p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     p9001-timeincident IS INITIAL.
    p9001-timeincident = '010000'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'ACT01'.
    w_changed = abap_true.
    LOOP AT tb_activity INTO ls_activity
         WHERE activity_code = p9001-activity_new.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR ls_activity.
    ENDIF.
    IF ls_activity-other_text IS NOT INITIAL.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-OTHER_ACTIVITY'.
    ELSEIF ls_activity-additional_info = 'D'.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-DRUG_CONT'.
    ELSEIF ls_activity-additional_info = 'M'.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-MENT_CONT'.
*    ELSEIF p9001-activity_new = '039'.
*      w_cursor = 'P9001-DV_CONT'.
    ELSE.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-CAUSE_NEW'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'CAS01'.
    w_changed = abap_true.
    LOOP AT tb_cause INTO ls_cause
         WHERE cause_code = p9001-cause_new.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR ls_cause.
    ENDIF.
    IF ls_cause-other_text IS NOT INITIAL.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-OTHER_CAUSE'.
    ELSE.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-WHERE_NEW'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'TAS01'.
    w_changed = abap_true.
    IF p9001-taser = '1'.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-TASER_CONT'.
    ELSE.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-LBV'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'LBV01'.
    IF p9001-lbv = '1'.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-LBV_CONT'.
    ELSE.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-GEL'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'GEL01'.
    w_changed = abap_true.
    IF p9001-gel = '1'.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-GEL_CONT'.
    ELSE.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-THIGH'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'THIGH01'.
    w_changed = abap_true.
    IF p9001-thigh = '1'.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-THIGH_CONT'.
    ELSE.
      w_cursor = 'P9001-DATEREPORTED'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1001  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_1002  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1002 INPUT.

  IF w_code2 = 'UPDINJ' OR
     w_code2 = 'DELINJ'.

    CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
      IMPORTING
        es_col_info = gr_outtab1_col_info
        es_row_no   = gr_outtab1_row_no.

    CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->get_selected_rows
      IMPORTING
        et_index_rows = gt_row_table
        et_row_no     = gt_row_no
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS        = 1.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines = 0.
      MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select an Injury to be Processed'.
    ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
      MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Injury'.
    ELSE.
      READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab1 INTO gr_outtab1 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
      gr_outtab1_row_table = gr_row_table.
      IF w_code2 = 'UPDINJ'.
        PERFORM call_7050 USING 'UPD'.
      ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DELINJ'.
        PERFORM call_7050 USING 'DEL'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'ADDINJ'.
    PERFORM call_7050 USING 'INS'.
  ENDIF.

*  IF w_code2 NE 'CANC' AND
*     w_code2 NE 'BACK' AND
*     w_code2 NE 'EXIT' AND
*     w_no_pernr IS INITIAL.
*
** set defaults for 1002
*    PERFORM set_defaults_1002.
*
** This is required to update the current field when a function
** code is selected.
**    CALL METHOD inc_inj_alv->check_changed_data.
**    CALL METHOD inc_nat_alv->check_changed_data.
*
*    IF w_code2(4) = 'INCI' OR
*      ( w_code2(4) = '%_GC' AND
*        w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter ).
*** Enter whilst in the ALV
*** Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
*      PERFORM validate_inc_inj CHANGING w_error.
*      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
*        PERFORM validate_inc_nat CHANGING w_error.
*      ENDIF.
*      PERFORM refresh_inc_inj_alv_total.
*      PERFORM refresh_inc_nat_alv_total.
*
*** If the user attempts to change tabs when an error is on
*** the screen, the system will allow it unless a MESSAGE error occurs
**** Unfortunately this stuffs up the second ALV table
**      IF w_error IS NOT INITIAL.
**        IF w_code2 IS NOT INITIAL.
**          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please correct the'
**                            'highlighted errors'.
**        ENDIF.
**      ENDIF.
**      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*
*  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1002  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  MODULE user_command_1003 INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1003 INPUT.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'TREAT01'.
      LOOP AT tb_treatopt INTO ls_treatopt
           WHERE treat_option = p9001-treatmentopt.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR ls_treatopt.
      ENDIF.

      w_changed = abap_true.
      IF ls_treatopt-medical_treat IS INITIAL.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-TREATMENTDOC'.
      ELSE.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-TREATMENTOPT'.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'AID'.
      w_changed = abap_true.
      IF p9001-treatfirsta = abap_true.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-TREATFIRSTPER'.
      ELSE.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-TREATMENTDESC'.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'FIT01'.
      w_changed = abap_true.
      LOOP AT tb_currfit INTO ls_currfit
           WHERE fitness_code = p9001-fitnessstat.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR ls_currfit.
      ENDIF.
      IF ls_currfit-return_work IS NOT INITIAL.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-FITNESSRETURN'.
      ELSEIF ls_currfit-med_review IS NOT INITIAL.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-DATE_MED_REV'.
      ELSE.
        w_cursor = 'P9001-FITNESSCERT'.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1003  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_1100  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1100 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_inc_sel.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab3 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab3 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'DISP1' OR 'UPD2' OR 'COPY1'.
      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab3_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab3_row_no.

      CALL METHOD inc_sel_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select an Incident to be Displayed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Incident'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab3 INTO gr_outtab3 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab3_row_table = gr_row_table.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 1100.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING w_pernr
                                    gr_outtab3-identnr.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD2'.
          PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING w_pernr.
          w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
          w_1000_from   = 1100.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING w_pernr
                                    gr_outtab3-identnr.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'COPY1'.
          CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm, p9001, w_canc.
          w_old_identnr   = gr_outtab3-identnr.
          PERFORM get_cop_rec.
*         CALL SCREEN 1200 STARTING AT 5 6 ENDING AT 125 23.
          IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
            PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING w_pernr.
            w_1000_action = 'COP'.
            w_1000_from   = 1100.
            PERFORM call_1000_a USING w_pernr
                                      gr_outtab3-identnr.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1100  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_1300  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1300 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_nm_sel.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab13 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab13 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'DISP1' OR 'UPD2' OR 'COPY1'.
      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab13_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab13_row_no.

      CALL METHOD nm_sel_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select an Incident to be Displayed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Incident'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab13 INTO gr_outtab13 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab13_row_table = gr_row_table.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 1300.
          PERFORM call_1000_b USING w_pernr
                                    gr_outtab13-identnr.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD2'.
          PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING w_pernr.
          w_1300_refresh = abap_true.
          IF w_history = abap_true.
            w_1000_action  = 'HIS'.
          ELSE.
            w_1000_action  = 'UNM'.
          ENDIF.
          w_1000_from    = 1300.
          PERFORM call_1000_b USING w_pernr
                                    gr_outtab13-identnr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1300  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_1200  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_1200 INPUT.

  CALL METHOD cop_rec_alv->check_changed_data.

  CASE w_popup_ucomm.
    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      w_canc = abap_true.
      REFRESH gt_outtab12.
      PERFORM free_cop_rec.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'SELALL'.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab12 INTO gr_outtab12.
        gr_outtab12-sel_code = abap_true.
        MODIFY gt_outtab12 FROM gr_outtab12.
      ENDLOOP.
      CALL METHOD cop_rec_alv->refresh_table_display.

    WHEN 'DELALL'.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab12 INTO gr_outtab12.
        gr_outtab12-sel_code = ' '.
        MODIFY gt_outtab12 FROM gr_outtab12.
      ENDLOOP.
      CALL METHOD cop_rec_alv->refresh_table_display.

    WHEN 'OK3'.
      PERFORM validate_1200.
      CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm, w_canc.
      PERFORM free_cop_rec.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'NEXT'.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_1200  INPUT

                                  gr_outtab4-incident.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2003  INPUT

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  MODULE change_full_case_mgmt INPUT
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE change_full_case_mgmt INPUT.
  CHECK zim_in_mast-full_case_mgmt IS INITIAL.

* check if External provider exists
  DATA: lv_full_case    TYPE boole_d.

  PERFORM check_full_case_management CHANGING lv_full_case.
  IF lv_full_case = abap_true.
    MESSAGE w999 WITH 'Case is fully managed Externally!'
                      'Unchecking the checkbox might cause'
                      'inconsistencies'.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                    "change_full_case_mgmt INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  TRANSITION_TO_EPSS  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE transition_to_epss INPUT.

  DATA: lv_answer TYPE char01,
        lw_in_cm  TYPE zim_in_cm.


  IF w_code2 = 'SAVE'.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm INTO lw_in_cm
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

    IF zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss NE lw_in_cm-transition_to_epss.

      IF zim_in_cm-wc_status EQ 'C'.
        CLEAR zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Closed incidents cannot be transitioned to EPSS!'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR: zim_in_cm-epss_last_changed_by,
             zim_in_cm-epss_last_changed_on,
             zim_in_cm-epss_last_changed_time.

      CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
        EXPORTING
          titlebar       = 'Warning Message'
          text_question  = 'The Transition to EPSS flag will be changed. Do you want to continue?'
          text_button_1  = 'Yes'(001)
          text_button_2  = 'No'(002)
          default_button = '2'
        IMPORTING
          answer         = lv_answer
        EXCEPTIONS
          text_not_found = 1
          OTHERS         = 2.

      CASE lv_answer.
        WHEN '1'.

*         " Set incident as EPSS and deactivate the (old) IP/Top Up record types.
          w_epss = abap_true.
*         create Record Types object for the incident
          IF gr_ip_rec IS INITIAL.
            CREATE OBJECT gr_ip_rec
              EXPORTING
                iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
                iv_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
          ENDIF.
          CALL METHOD gr_ip_rec->deactivate_ip_records( ).

*         Change control for audit
          zim_in_cm-epss_last_changed_by = sy-uname.
          zim_in_cm-epss_last_changed_on = sy-datum.
          zim_in_cm-epss_last_changed_time = sy-uzeit.

          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The transition to EPSS flag has been changed!'.

        WHEN '2'.
          IF zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss EQ abap_true.
            CLEAR: zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss.
          ELSE.
            zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss = abap_true.
          ENDIF.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The transition to EPSS flag has not been changed!'.
        WHEN OTHERS.
          IF zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss EQ abap_true.
            CLEAR:zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss.
          ELSE.
            zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss = abap_true.
          ENDIF.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The transition to EPSS flag has not been changed!'.
      ENDCASE.

    ENDIF. " if zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss NE w_in_cm-transition_to_epss.
  ENDIF.  "SAVE

ENDMODULE.                 " TRANSITION_TO_EPSS  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_4102  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_4102 OUTPUT.

* Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF clm_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT clm_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'CLM_SEL_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

* create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT clm_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = clm_sel_container
        i_appl_events = 'X'.

*--Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

*--Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3404
                FOR clm_sel_alv.

*--Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

* provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

* inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    clm_sel_layout-no_toolbar = 'X'.
    clm_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    clm_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    clm_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    clm_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_clm_sel_epss TABLES clm_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES clm_sel_functions.

    REFRESH clm_sel_style.
    CLEAR   clm_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = clm_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = clm_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = clm_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab30.

    w_4102_refresh = 'X'.

  ENDIF.


  IF w_4102_refresh = 'X'.
    PERFORM get_claim_status_epss.

    CLEAR w_4102_refresh.
    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = clm_sel_layout.
    clm_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = 'X'.
    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = clm_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD clm_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_4102  OUTPUT

*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_1006 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF inc_bp_inj_container IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT inc_bp_inj_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'INC_BP_INJ_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT inc_bp_inj_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = inc_bp_inj_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    inc_bp_inj_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    inc_bp_inj_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    inc_bp_inj_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    inc_bp_inj_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    inc_bp_inj_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_inc_bp_inj TABLES inc_bp_inj_fieldcat.

    REFRESH inc_bp_inj_style.
    CLEAR   inc_bp_inj_style.

    CALL METHOD inc_bp_inj_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = inc_bp_inj_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = inc_bp_inj_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = inc_bp_inj_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab58.


  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_1006  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_7070  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_7070 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_7070_action = 'DEL'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

*    IF w_zp902_location-other_text IS INITIAL AND
*       screen-group1 = '100'.
*      screen-active = '0'.
*      screen-invisible = '1'.
*      MODIFY SCREEN.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    IF w_zp902_nature-other_text IS INITIAL AND
*       screen-group1 = '200'.
*      screen-active = '0'.
*      screen-invisible = '1'.
*      MODIFY SCREEN.
*    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_7070  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHECK_SCREEN_7070  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_screen_7070 INPUT.

  IF w_popup_ucomm = 'BACK' OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'CANC' OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'EXIT' OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'DELETE'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* To make the screen dynamic and have the other field displayed
* automatically, the tables must be read.
* But if the validations are performed, error messages will
* appear immediately i.e. enter location and the nature must
* be entered will appear.
  IF w_popup_ucomm = 'LOC'  OR
     w_popup_ucomm = 'NAT'.
    PERFORM read_7070.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM validate_7070.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_7070  INPUT
